# Execution package

Generated by StackToIT from `execution-plan.json`. Nothing in this folder has been run: StackToIT reads and writes files and creates, changes and deletes nothing in any cloud. Every step is carried out by a person, by OpenTofu or by a script of this folder that a person runs. Paths are relative to the output folder.

Partially executable migration package · Proposal only · Not approved.

**What will be created.** OpenTofu creates 53 resource blocks; the scripts of this package make 4 STACKIT API calls. 11 source resources need no STACKIT resource. Architect work: 254 manual steps in 41 work packages.

**What can run now.** 48 of the 57 automated steps (OpenTofu or a script) can run now (1 waits for a manual prerequisite, 8 require a design decision) · 9 of those 48 rest on a default you have not confirmed (public endpoint, size derived from the source); a person can start 114 of the 254 manual steps now (4 wait for a manual prerequisite, 71 require a design decision, 65 are not executable as the plan stands); 3 steps are permanently unsupported (refused); 1 step has its verification incomplete: it carries critical settings of the source that no check validates, which a person confirms in the draft.

**Readiness levels.** 57 automated steps can be generated · 48 can run now · 39 are ready for migration (they rest on no default that is not confirmed) · 0 are approved (every default and decision they rest on is confirmed in the decisions file). 9 steps can run now and wait only for the confirmation of a default (DEC-GEN-CONVERT-EXPOSURE-WIDENED, DEC-GEN-CONVERT-SIZE-FROM-SOURCE): confirm the default, or choose another option, in decisions.yaml.

**What the figures count.** Three measures, not one: "can run now" means the preconditions of the step in the plan are met (48 of the 57 automated steps); "automation-eligible" means the critical settings of the unit are closed (0 of 44 units; 17 more become eligible once their open items are answered; 27 cannot be automated in this build: a critical setting STACKIT does not support (13 units) and 4 more reasons, every one on the Automation eligibility line of index.html); "actually runnable" means the values the step reads are supplied as well (0 of the 48 steps that can run now: the plan still lists 43 OpenTofu variables, the STACKIT project id among them, as needing a value).

**What cannot be preserved.** 307 critical settings of 48 source resources that the draft does not carry are open as manual or refused steps ([engineering/not-carried.csv](../engineering/not-carried.csv) lists what the draft does not carry, property by property). 3 steps are refused: the target has no equivalent (see What cannot be migrated).

**Decisions required.** 9 decisions (17 questions) must be answered before the steps that wait for them can be carried out ([Required decisions](#required-decisions); described in [executive/decisions.md](../executive/decisions.md)).

**First work package.** [`WP-02`](manual/actions.md), Set up the identities and access (prepare) — owner: Identity Architect, 10 steps. The next package that waits, `WP-08` (Write the security group rules the draft does not write (security)), waits for decisions (10 of 13 steps). It opens when `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.carts_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.catalog_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.checkout_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.orders_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.ui_service.aws_security_group.this` are answered.

## How the package is carried out

This is an operator-executable migration plan: a person, OpenTofu or a script that a person runs carries out each step, in the order below, and nothing in this folder runs by itself. OpenTofu applies the OpenTofu steps together in one `tofu apply`, so the manual steps and STACKIT API calls that come before it are not enforced: `execution/terraform/apply.md` lists them as a checklist to complete first. An executor that would carry the plan out step by step (`stacktoit execute`) is designed and not implemented.

## Verdict

**Partially executable migration package**

Steps: partially executable: some steps cannot be carried out as the plan stands, whatever is decided: they are marked not executable under Execution order.

The verdict is of the 314 steps of this package. Every resource and data block of the draft (53) is a step of it. Every source resource of the assessment (52) is a step of it, or needs neither a STACKIT resource nor a step (0). Of them, 11 need no STACKIT resource (`terraform/README.md` lists them under Not needed on STACKIT); 11 of them still have a step of the plan. These files of the draft hold no step of the plan at all: `terraform/terraform.tf`, `terraform/variables.tf`.

Execution completeness: COMPLETE (each of the 254 manual steps states what it is, why, who carries it out and when it is done, and the input it needs where it depends on one).

48 of the 57 automated steps (OpenTofu or a script) can run now (1 waits for a manual prerequisite, 8 require a design decision) · 9 of those 48 rest on a default you have not confirmed (public endpoint, size derived from the source); a person can start 114 of the 254 manual steps now (4 wait for a manual prerequisite, 71 require a design decision, 65 are not executable as the plan stands); 3 steps are permanently unsupported (refused); 1 step has its verification incomplete: it carries critical settings of the source that no check validates, which a person confirms in the draft. Migration ready: NO — 307 critical settings of 48 source resources have manual or refused steps open (293 manual, 14 refused); 147 steps cannot be carried out now (79 wait only for a decision, 65 are not executable whatever is decided, 3 are refused).

The critical settings of the source that the draft does not carry, by source resource: each is a manual or refused step under Execution order (a source resource the draft writes no resource for carries them in its own step); `assurance/critical-semantics.json` says why each is open and what closes it. A setting the knowledge base names by a generated id is said in words before its id: the source attribute it is, as the step of the setting states it, or else its id in words where the plan states no attribute for it.

A redesign could close it once for the estate by one of these alternatives: confirm that the estate has one VPC (the project the draft creates is then that VPC's boundary, and the setting closes); give each source VPC a STACKIT project of its own, joined through a Network Area where their networks must reach each other; or keep one project and attach each group only to the interfaces of the networks that replace its VPC, with no rule's remote_security_group_id naming a group whose members sit in a network the source VPC kept apart. Fill in `resolution` (`RESOLVED_BY_ARCHITECT`, `ACCEPTED_DIFFERENCE`, `ACCEPTED_LOSS`, `ACCEPTED_REDESIGN`, or `REJECTED` when the proposal is not accepted: the decision then stays open), `reviewed_by` (the role that answers), `reviewed_at` (the date), `rationale`, `evidence_refs` (at least one reference for an answer that accepts) and, for a record that states options, `chosen_option`; keep `id`, `members`, `members_sha256` and `artifact_sha256` as the template gives them. Then run `stacktoit assess --decisions FILE` on the same source: an answer frees only the steps its effect allows, and a freed step says which answer freed it.

## Required credentials

- OpenTofu: the STACKIT provider reads its credentials from the environment, for example `STACKIT_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY_PATH` (the path of a service account key file); the account needs the roles that create the resources of the steps. `terraform/README.md` says what the draft needs.

## Execution order

The steps in the order they are carried out: a step comes after every step it depends on. A step is named here without the name of the generator that made it; `execution-plan.json` holds the full id of each.

A text that many steps share word for word is written once here, with what is the step's own in braces (`{name}` for the name of a resource, `{address}` for an IP address or network, `{steps}` for the steps a state names). A line below names the wording of what its step does (W) and the state it is in (S), and gives after "Where" what stands for the braces.

- **S1** (39 steps): not executable as the plan stands: StackToIT performs none of this: it deletes nothing in any cloud, and no step of this plan automates the retirement; it also waits for `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-API-OPT-IN`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-DATA-MOVEMENT/module.dependencies.module.catalog_opensearch.aws_opensearch_domain.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-ENGINE-VERSION-DOWNGRADE`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-DATA-MOVEMENT/module.dependencies.module.catalog_rds.aws_rds_cluster.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-DATA-MOVEMENT/module.dependencies.module.checkout_elasticache_redis.aws_elasticache_replication_group.default`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-DATA-MOVEMENT/module.dependencies.module.dynamodb_carts.aws_dynamodb_table.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-DATA-MOVEMENT/module.dependencies.module.orders_rds.aws_rds_cluster.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-AMBIGUOUS-INSTANCE-SELECTION`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-INGRESS-EXPOSURE-PATH`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.carts_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.catalog_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.checkout_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.orders_service.aws_security_group.this`, `DEC-GEN-CONVERT-POD-SEGMENTATION/module.retail_app_ecs.module.ui_service.aws_security_group.this`

140. The data preflight of module.dependencies.module.checkout_elasticache_redis.aws_elasticache_replication_group.default (`MAN/data/module.dependencies.module.checkout_elasticache_redis.aws_elasticache_replication_group.default/preflight`) — manual step: Close what the plan lists as UNKNOWN, from the move host, with read-only commands; no password goes on a command line or into the history of the shell. Source (`valkey-cli -h \<source-host> -p \<source-port> --tls`, without --tls where the source has none): `INFO server` (the engine and its version), `INFO keyspace` and `DBSIZE` (the keys and the keys with an expiry; on every primary when cluster mode is on, listed with `CLUSTER NODES`), `INFO memory` (used_memory and maxmemory), `SCAN 0 COUNT 100` with `TYPE`, `DUMP` and `PTTL` of a few keys of each family (the commands of the copy; the types the copy has no branch for show here), and the credential the copy signs in with. Target (after the draft is applied and the instance and a service key exist; `valkey-cli -u rediss://\<target-user>@\<target-host>:\<target-port> --tls --sni \<target-host>`): `INFO server` (Valkey 8), `DBSIZE` (0) and `INFO memory`; do not expect `CONFIG`, `ACL` or `MODULE`, which a probe found denied to the service key. Check that the move host is in `parameters.sgw_acl` of the instance (remove it afterwards), reaches both ends, and that the clients of the cache are listed with the TLS and the credential each uses. Runbook section: `terraform/handover/cache-dependencies_checkout_elasticache_redis_default/data-move.md#preflight`. (`terraform/handover/cache-dependencies_checkout_elasticache_redis_default/data-move.md`) After steps 138, 139. (executable: a person can start it now)
141. The data rehearsal of module.dependencies.module.checkout_elasticache_redis.aws_elasticache_replication_group.default (`MAN/data/module.dependencies.module.checkout_elasticache_redis.aws_elasticache_replication_group.default/rehearsal`) — manual step: Rehearse the copy on a sample into a scratch Key Value Store instance of the same plan and version as the draft's instance (a second instance, deleted afterwards: the real instance must stay empty for the final copy): copy the keys of a prefix or a few thousand keys of every family with the client of the copy (`SCAN` with `MATCH`, `DUMP`, `PTTL`, `RESTORE` with the remaining time to live), note the keys a second, the bytes a second and the keys RESTORE refuses, point one copy of the application at the scratch instance with TLS and run its own test suite, and compute the freeze window from the throughput: keys / (keys a second) is the lower bound of the copy. Delete the scratch instance afterwards. Runbook section: `terraform/handover/cache-dependencies_checkout_elasticache_redis_default/data-move.md#rehearsal`. After step 140. (executable once its manual prerequisite is done: it waits for step 140, a manual prerequisite)

Source (`mysql --host=\<source-host> --port=\<source-port> --user=\<source-user> --password`): `SELECT VERSION();`, `SELECT table_schema, table_collation, COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables GROUP BY 1, 2;` and the same over `information_schema.columns` for the collations the columns use, `SELECT routine_schema, routine_type, COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.routines GROUP BY 1, 2;` and the triggers, events and views the same way, the DEFINER accounts (`SELECT DISTINCT definer FROM information_schema.routines`, and for the views and events), `SELECT user, host, plugin FROM mysql.user ORDER BY 1, 2;` and `SHOW GRANTS FOR '\<user>'@'\<host>';` for each (names and plugins, never the authentication string), `SELECT @@GLOBAL.sql_mode, @@GLOBAL.time_zone, @@GLOBAL.gtid_mode;`, `SHOW DATABASES;` and the engines and the tables without a primary key (the queries of the check table-engines-and-keys). Target (after the draft is applied and the instance and its service key exist; `mysql --host=\<target-host> --port=\<target-port> --user=\<target-user> --password --database=\<target-database>`): `SELECT VERSION();`, `SELECT @@GLOBAL.sql_mode, @@GLOBAL.time_zone;`, `SHOW VARIABLES LIKE 'character_set%';`, `SHOW VARIABLES LIKE 'collation%';`, `SHOW COLLATION WHERE Collation LIKE 'utf8mb4%0900%';` and `SHOW GRANTS;`. Check that the move host is in `parameters.sgw_acl` of the instance (remove it afterwards), reaches both ends and uses the custom port of the instance's service key. Runbook section: `terraform/handover/database-dependencies_catalog_rds_this/data-move.md#preflight`. (`terraform/handover/database-dependencies_catalog_rds_this/data-move.md`) After steps 178, 179. Delete the scratch instance and the dump afterwards. Runbook section: `terraform/handover/database-dependencies_catalog_rds_this/data-move.md#rehearsal`. After step 180. The target instance must be empty before the load (the STACKIT how-to of the restore says so): load the dump with the mysql client, as the user of the instance's service key, into the empty instance: `mysql --host=\<target-host> --port=\<target-port> --user=\<target-user> --password --database=\<target-database> < \<dump-file>.sql 2> load.log`, and read every line of `load.log`. A DEFINER account that does not exist on the target, a collation the target does not know and a database the target does not have are the errors the checks name: each is settled by a person, never by skipping the object. ## Terraform commands

`execution/terraform/apply.md` lists every OpenTofu step with its resource and file. OpenTofu applies the whole draft in one run and enforces none of the manual steps, STACKIT API calls or phases of the plan: complete the steps that `execution/terraform/apply.md` lists under "Complete these steps before tofu apply" first. 4 OpenTofu steps are not executable yet: a full apply creates every block of the draft as it stands, theirs included, so apply only when every OpenTofu step is executable. From the output folder:

```sh
cd terraform
tofu init
tofu validate
tofu plan -out=stackit.tfplan
tofu apply stackit.tfplan
```

## API commands

Each call has a request file (the call as data) and a script that renders it. A script is run with `bash <script>` from any folder; it stops before the call when its step is not executable, when an input is not set, or when the call needs a confirmation.

The execution order names, as "Step state", how far the step of a call has come: "generated, not yet tested offline" (a converter wrote it and no test ran its script against a local stub), "generated, tested offline" (a test of StackToIT ran the script under a real shell against a local stub), "verified live" (a recorded probe against the STACKIT API shows the step does what it states) and "catalogued, not generated" (the call is known and described and no converter writes it). StackToIT runs none of these calls itself.

A script compares the answer of its call with the answer the plan expects (the HTTP status exactly, and a member when the plan names one). It stops with exit 3 when its step is not executable, 4 when the call needs the confirmation, 5 when an input, a tool or a file is missing or unsafe, 6 when its lookup cannot tell whether the object exists, 7 when the answer is not the expected one, and 8 when a postcondition it checks does not hold.

A script that calls a STACKIT API with curl trusts the certificate authorities curl trusts. For an API behind a private authority, set `STACKIT_CA_BUNDLE` to a PEM file of its certificates (curl's --cacert); the certificate is always verified. Each call ends after `STACKIT_CALL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` seconds (120 unless it names others, at most 3600), so an API that does not answer stops the script with a non-zero exit instead of holding it; a call that timed out may still have been made, so read the target before running the script again.

A script that writes a secret into STACKIT Secrets Manager (the Vault-compatible API) logs in as the Secrets Manager user the draft creates, with the username and password that user has once the draft is applied (the script reads them from the draft's outputs with tofu output, or from its `STACKIT_INPUT_USERNAME` and `STACKIT_INPUT_PASSWORD` variables), and writes the document of key/value pairs that the file named in its `STACKIT_INPUT_SECRET_DOCUMENT_<n>` variable holds. The document is a file of yours: the package holds no value of any secret, and the script prints no value, puts none on a command line and keeps none outside its own temporary folder. A secret that already has a current version stops the script (exit 4) until `STACKIT_CONFIRM_REPLACE` is set to the path of the secret, because the write makes a new version the current one. The user is a migration user with write access to every secret of the instance and its password is in the OpenTofu state: delete the user (remove its block from the draft and apply) once the secrets are written.

## Manual steps

254 steps are carried out by a person, each described in `execution/manual/actions.md`: what, why, who, when, what it needs and when it is done. Roles named: Data Architect, Identity Architect, Migration Owner, Network Architect, Platform Architect, Security Architect.

## Verification

`execution/verify/checks.md` lists 283 checks to hold before the steps and 65 checks to hold after them, one checklist row each. 1 of them confirms a critical setting the draft carries (verification only): a person confirms it in the file of the draft, and no step sets it up by hand.

## Rollback

Undoing a step: 68 can be undone (the step says how), 10 only in part (what was written since is lost), 231 by a person, 5 cannot be undone. From the cutover on, steps 198, 199, 200, 201, 202, 203, 204, 205, 206, 207, 208, 209, 210, 211, 212, 213, 214, 215, 216, 217, 218, 219, 220, 221, 222, 223, 224, 225, 226, 227, 228, 229, 230, 231, 232, 233, 235, 236, 237, 238, 239, 240, 241, 242, 243, 258, 259, 260, 261, 262, 263, 264, 265, 266, 267, 268, 269, 270, 271, 272, 273, 274, 275, 276, 277, 278, 279, 280, 281, 282, 283, 284, 285, 286, 287, 288, 289, 290, 291, 292, 293, 294, 295, 296, 297, 298, 299, 300, 301, 302, 303, 304, 305, 306, 307, 308, 309, 310, 311, 312, 313, 314 cannot be undone in full by a tool: decide how before the cutover starts. Each step's rollback is in the actions sheet and, for a manual step, in `execution/manual/actions.md`.

## Files of this package

- `execution-plan.json` — the plan itself, machine-readable, at the root of the run: the one file of the plan (issue #317: the package holds no copy of it)
- `execution/terraform/apply.md` — the OpenTofu steps and commands
- `execution/api/` — one request file and one script for each STACKIT API call
- `execution/manual/actions.md` — the manual steps and what is not migrated
- `execution/verify/checks.md` — the checks before and after each step
- `migration/execution-actions.csv` — one row per step, for a spreadsheet
- `migration/work-packages.csv` — one row per work package of the manual steps, for a spreadsheet
